# LP candidates: portable theorems of the IDLA / mated-CRT repository

Scope. Theorems proved in this repository (import cone of `MatedCRT.Audit.CountedAxioms`) whose statement uses no constant declared in a `MatedCRT.*` module. `MatedCRT.LPStaging` is excluded (already mirrored in LatticeProb). Props and definitions are excluded (theorems only). Constants of `LatticeProb` and of `MatedCRT.LPStaging` count as LatticeProb constants, the allowed package; constants of `HarmonicBalls` (and any other package) make a candidate non-portable.

Method. A Lean probe (`notes/z12-lp-candidates.lean`, outside the repository, fleet path `/home/nitro/paper-fleets/mcrt/notes/`) walks `env.constants` of the import cone, keeps the theorems in `MatedCRT.*` modules, and tests `Expr.getUsedConstants` of the type. For the proof, it computes the transitive closure of `MatedCRT.*` constants (types and values), skipping elaborator auxiliaries (`_proof_N`, `_simp_N`, `_auxLemma`, `match_N`, `eq_N`, `_unary`, `_binary`, `proof_N`). Run log: `logs/lc/120418-3402779.log` (fleet). Declaration lines come from `findDeclarationRanges?`.

Definitions used in the tables:
- `closure`: number of `MatedCRT.*` constants in the proof closure (0 = portable as is).
- `class PA`: statement and proof closure use no `HarmonicBalls` constant; closure 0.
- `class PL`: as PA, closure 1 to 50 (the needed `MatedCRT.*` lemmas are listed in the table, column "direct paper deps").
- `class HB`: statement or proof closure uses a `HarmonicBalls` constant (excluded).

## Counts

| step | count |
|---|---|
| theorems in `MatedCRT.*` modules of the cone (excluding LPStaging and elaborator auxiliaries) | 8189 |
| of these, statement uses no `MatedCRT.*` constant | 1816 |
| of these, class HB (statement or proof uses a HarmonicBalls constant) | 547 |
| class PA (portable as is: closure 0) | 935 |
| class PL (portable with `MatedCRT.*` lemmas, closure 1 to 50) | 334 |
| class PX (closure above 50) | 0 |
| of PA + PL, private declarations (`_private`, not citable from outside) | 8 |

Of the 1816 statement-free theorems, 1202 have closure 0; of those, 935 are class PA and the other 267 use a HarmonicBalls constant in the proof. Table rows: PA + PL minus private = 1261 (appendix).

## Topic counts (portable pool PA + PL, 1269 incl. 8 private)

Keyword rule on the printed statement, first match in this order: Gaussian (`Gaussian`, `gaussian`, `Covariance`, `IsGaussian`, `GFF`); Brownian/exit (`Brownian`, `exit`, `hitting`, `stopping`, `killed`, `Markov`); Green/potential (`Green`, `harmonic`, `potential`, `Dirichlet`, `Laplac`, `Poisson`, `obstacle`, `Harnack`, `maximum principle`); measure/measurability (`Measurable`, `Measure`, `Integrable`, `Integral`, `σ`); else real analysis / other. This is a crude rule: it sorts names, it does not judge reusability.

| topic | PA | PL | total |
|---|---|---|---|
| Gaussian fields | 18 | 6 | 24 |
| Brownian motion and exit times | 20 | 16 | 36 |
| Green functions and potential theory | 20 | 16 | 36 |
| measure theory and measurability | 279 | 155 | 434 |
| real analysis and other | 598 | 141 | 739 |

Most portable rows are in `MatedCRT/Proofs` (543) and `MatedCRT/FirstPass` (369); module-level counts are in the appendix (column file).

## Groups (hand-checked from names and printed statements)

These are the groups I read; the rest of the table is machine-listed only.

- Gaussian fields. `MatedCRT/Proofs/Field/WholePlaneBrownian.lean`: `Field.brownian_variance` (PA), `Field.brownian_covariance` (PL, closure 6), `Field.covariance_congr_ae'` (PA; covariance of a.e.-equal variables). `Field/GaussClosure` (9 rows): `GaussClosure.variance_of_map_eq` (PA), `map_eq_gaussianReal_of_tendsto_variance` (PL, closure 4), `exists_map_eq_gaussianReal_of_tendsto_ae` (PL, closure 7). Sec3 KilledOccupation: `gaussianReal_Ioo_le_inv_sqrt` (PA; bound on the `gaussianReal` mass of an open interval, variance T entering the bound; statement read only up to the bound).
- Brownian motion and exit times. `MatedCRT/FirstPass/LPBridge/ExitRestartLimit.lean`: `ExitRestart.measurable_eval_at` (PA), `exitTimeTrunc_eq_toNNReal_min` (PA; `MarkovProcess.ContinuousPath.exitTimeTrunc`), `measurable_exitTimeTrunc` (PL, closure 1). `Proofs/Sec3/KilledOccupation/ClockFinite.lean`: `measurable_path_eval` and `measurable_clockIntegral` (PA). `Proofs/Probability/BrownianMaxCompat.lean`: `ae_forall_eq_of_modification` (PA), `le_four_mul_of_one_sub_two_mul_le` (PA), `measure_exists_abs_sub_ge_le` (PL, closure 8). Overlap with LP `Prob/BrownianMax` to be checked before any copy.
- Green functions and potential theory. Laplacian calculus (Armitage-Gardiner, harmonic functions): `continuous_laplacian_of_contDiff` (`FirstPass/S1c/TestLocal.lean`, PA), `laplacian_eq_zero_of_eventually_zero` (same file, PA), `laplacian_eq_zero_off_tsupport` (`FirstPass/S2g/AllDisks.lean`, PA), `laplacian_comp_add_const` (`Proofs/Sec3/UniformConvergenceGreenKernel/HarmonicTranslation.lean`, PA), `laplacian_comp_add_right` (`Proofs/Sec3/ConvergenceNormalizedExitTime/LaplacianTranslation.lean`, PA). Logarithmic potential: `Proofs/Infra/LogKernel.lean`: `measure_ball_lt_top_of_vol` (PA), `ofReal_log_le_tsum_indicator` (PA; bound of `log(1/|z-w|)` by a sum of indicators).
- Measure theory and measurability. `Proofs/Measurability/GoodScale.lean`: `measurable_iSup_logPotential` (PL, closure 3), `measurable_iInf_measure_ball_of_isCompact` (PL, closure 2). `Proofs/Sec1/EnvironmentScaling/RadialLocalIntegrability.lean`: `integrableOn_one_add_abs_log_norm_closedBall` (PL, closure 2), `locallyIntegrable_of_continuousOn_compl_zero` (PL, closure 3). `Proofs/Sec1/EnvironmentScaling/MeasurableAngularIntegrals.lean` (9 M rows) and `circleAverage_eq_normalized` (PA; `Real.circleAverage` in polar form).
- Low reuse. `Proofs/LBM/DirichletDiscBarrier.lean` (`re_cpow_real_le`, `cos_half_pi_mul_pos`; elementary, Mathlib-adjacent). `FirstPass/S6r` and `FirstPass/S6n` (paper-specific, mostly R rows).

## Flags (read before copying anything)

1. Paper-named statements in LatticeProb. 11 portable rows are named `MatedCRTHarmonic.*` (for example `MatedCRTHarmonic.MatedCRT.ae_locMinDistinct`, PL, closure 30, in `Proofs/MatingOfTrees/BrownianLocalMin.lean`). Their statements use constants of LatticeProb module `LatticeProb/Bonanno/MatedCRTHarmonic/Frozen/MatedCRTTwoSidedMin.lean` (for example `MatedCRTHarmonic.MatedCRT.twoSidedMin`). The rule admits them (constants of LatticeProb), but the names are paper-specific and the LatticeProb package used by the build already contains them (under `Bonanno/MatedCRTHarmonic`). Operator decision needed: they break the "no paper-specific names" rule for LP staging.
2. Private declarations (8 rows, `_private.MatedCRT.Proofs.MatingOfTrees.BrownianLocalMin...`) are not citable from another module. They are excluded from the appendix table.
3. `MatedCRT/Proposed/DuplantierMillerSheffieldCone2014.lean` (`Proposed.lqgQ_sub_pos_of_lt_two`, PA) is in an unfrozen Proposed module: not a candidate. `MatedCRT/Audit/StatementsSec3Sec5M1.lean` (10 PA rows) are audit statements: not candidates.
4. Portable is a constant-level test only. Many PA rows are paper-specific by content (names such as `igPullback`, `affineReroot`, `ConeCircle`). Reusability was judged by name and printed statement for the groups above only.
5. Description column. The appendix gives the printed Lean statement (truncated to about 110 characters), not a hand-written one-line description; hand descriptions exist for the groups above only.

## Ranked short list (most reusable first)

1. Laplacian calculus: `continuous_laplacian_of_contDiff`, `laplacian_comp_add_const`, `laplacian_comp_add_right`, `laplacian_eq_zero_off_tsupport` (PA; harmonic functions, Armitage-Gardiner).
2. Logarithmic potentials: `Proofs/Infra/LogKernel.lean` (PA; log kernel bounds, ball masses).
3. Gaussian closure and Gaussian limits: `Field/GaussClosure` (PA and PL; `gaussianReal` limits, Duplantier-Sheffield GFF terminology).
4. Brownian covariance and variance: `Field/WholePlaneBrownian.lean` (PA and PL; `MarkovProcess.brownianMotion`).
5. Exit times of continuous paths: `FirstPass/LPBridge/ExitRestartLimit.lean` (PA and PL; `MarkovProcess.ContinuousPath.exitTimeTrunc`).
6. Killed occupation measurability: `Proofs/Sec3/KilledOccupation/ClockFinite.lean` (PA; path evaluation and clock integral measurability).
7. Brownian maximum and modification: `Proofs/Probability/BrownianMaxCompat.lean` (PA and PL; check overlap with LP `Prob/BrownianMax` first).
8. Measurability of log potentials and ball masses: `Proofs/Measurability/GoodScale.lean` (PL).
9. Local integrability of log singularities: `Proofs/Sec1/EnvironmentScaling/RadialLocalIntegrability.lean` (PL).
10. Polar coordinates and circle averages: `Proofs/Sec1/EnvironmentScaling/MeasurableAngularIntegrals.lean` (PA and PL; `Real.circleAverage`).
11. Measurability of circle and cone pairings: `Proofs/Measurability/CirclePairing.lean`, `ConePairing.lean` (PA).
12. Gaussian covariance congruence: `covariance_congr_ae'` (`Field/WholePlaneBrownian.lean`) and `ConeMeanZeroRestriction.covariance_congr_ae` (PA).
13. Dirichlet disc barrier estimates: `Proofs/LBM/DirichletDiscBarrier.lean` (PA; low reuse, Mathlib overlap likely).
14. (Not recommended as a group) `MatedCRTHarmonic.*` rows: paper-named, see flag 1.
15. (Not recommended as a group) real-analysis lemmas in `FirstPass/S6r`, `FirstPass/S6n`: paper-specific.
